var TJHeaderBidding = function(params) {
	var Self = this;

	Self.generateGUID = function() {
		function s4() {
			return Math.floor((1 + Math.random()) * 0x10000).toString(16).substring(1);
		}
		return s4() + s4() + '-' + s4() + '-' + s4() + '-' + s4() + '-' + s4() + s4() + s4();
	};

	Self.prepareUrl = function(platform, zone_ids, guid) {
		if (platform == "mobile_touch") {
			var i=0;
				j = zone_ids.length,
				dataObj = {
					spots: []
				},
				data =  [];

			for (;i<j;i++){
				dataObj.spots.push({"zone": zone_ids[i]});
			}

			data.push(dataObj);

			return Self.params.url_mobile + "&data=" + encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(data)) + '&hb=' + guid;
		} else {
			return Self.params.url + "&zone_id=" + zone_ids.join(',') + '&hb=' + guid;
		}
	};

	Self.filterIDs = function(zone_list, filters){
		//filter zone IDs
		var zone_ids = [],
			zones = (typeof filters[Self.params.page_name] == "undefined") ? filters['default'] : filters[Self.params.page_name],
			zones_length = zones.length,
			i = 0;

		for (;i<zones_length; i++) {
			zone_ids.push(Self.params.zones_list[zones[i]]);
		}

		if ((Self.params.preroll_zone_id != null)
			&& (typeof Self.params.preroll_zone_id[Self.params.platform] != 'undefined')
			&& (Self.params.preroll_zone_id[Self.params.platform] != null)) {
			zone_ids.push(Self.params.preroll_zone_id[Self.params.platform]);
		}

		return zone_ids;
	};

	Self.makeRequest = function(requestType) {
		if (Self.zone_ids.length == 0) return;

		if (requestType == 'script') {
			var el = document.createElement('script'),
			s = document.getElementsByTagName('script')[0];

			el.type = 'text/javascript';
			el.async = true;
			el.src = Self.url,

			s.parentNode.insertBefore(el, s);
			return;
		}

		if (requestType == 'pixel') {
			var img = document.createElement("img"),
				elBody = document.getElementsByTagName("BODY")[0];

			img.setAttribute('src', Self.url);
			if (elBody !== null) {
				elBody.insertBefore(img, elBody.firstChild);
			}
			return;
		}

		if ((requestType == 'xhr') && (typeof XMLHttpRequest != "undefined")) {
			var a = new XMLHttpRequest();
			a.open("GET", Self.url, true);
			a.withCredentials = true;
			a.send(null);

		}
	};

	Self.process = function(params) {
		Self.params = params;
		Self.zone_ids = (params.filter_zones) ? Self.filterIDs(params.zones_list, params.filter_array[Self.params.platform]) : params.zones_list;
		Self.url = Self.prepareUrl(params.platform, Self.zone_ids, Self.params.guid);
		Self.makeRequest(params.type); //'script', 'xhr' or 'pixel'
	};

	Self.getHB = function() {
		return (Self.zone_ids.length == 0) ? "" : "&hb=" + Self.params.guid;
	};

	return Self.process(params);
};

                            console.log('Service Worker registration failed: ', err);
                        });

                    if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
                        navigator.serviceWorker.ready.then(function(reg) {
                            reg.pushManager.subscribe({
                                userVisibleOnly: true
                            }).then(function(sub) {

                            }).catch(function(e) {
                                if (Notification.permission === 'denied') {
                                    console.warn('Permission for notifications was denied');
                                } else {
                                    console.error('Unable to subscribe to push', e);
                                }
                            });
                        })
                    }
                }

<script src="//ss.phncdn.com/networkbar-4.0.3.js" type="text/javascript"></script>
<script>
    // 4k styles for networkbar
    (function () {
        var styles = document.head.querySelectorAll('style'),
            i = 0,
            len = styles.length,
            phStyle,
            str;

        for (; i < len; i++) {
            if (styles[i].innerHTML.indexOf('#phPxToRem') > -1) {
                phStyle = styles[i];
                break;
            }
        }

        if (!phStyle) {
            return false;
        }

        str = phStyle.innerHTML;
        str = str.replace(/([[0-9]['px'])\w+/g, function (chars) {
            chars = chars.replace('px', 'rem');
            return '.' + chars;
        });

        phStyle.innerHTML = str;

    }());
</script>
<script type="text/javascript">
    var ua = window.navigator.userAgent,
        isLegacy = ua.match(/MSIE 8|MSIE 7/),
        topLoginID = document.getElementById("top-login"),
        loginDivID = document.getElementById("logindiv");

    if (!page_params.isLoggedIn) {
        if (!page_params.mgModal) {
            page_params.mgModal = true;
        }

        if (topLoginID) {
            topLoginID.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                e.stopPropagation();
            }, false);
        }

        if (loginDivID) {
            loginDivID.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                e.stopPropagation();
            }, false);
        }

    }

    // show adblock message on all pages when cookie "adBlockClosed" is not set
    var re = new RegExp("adBlockClosed=([^;]+)");
    var adBlockClosedCookie = re.exec(document.cookie);
    if (adBlockClosedCookie == null) {  //if cookie not set, show adBlock message
        var IS_ADBLOCK = function () {
            "use strict";
            var Self = this;

            /**
             * Init method to set up more vars or initialite methods inside the object
             */
            Self.init = function (options) {
                Self.params = options;
                page_params.holiday_promo ? "" : Self.adBlockAlert();
            };

            Self.adBlockAlert = function () {
                var $adBlockAlertWrapper = document.getElementById(Self.params.wrapper),
                    $msg = document.getElementById(Self.params.alert),
                    $adBlockAlertClose = document.getElementById("adBlockAlertClose");

                $adBlockAlertWrapper.className = "adBlockAlertWrapper";
                $msg.style.display = 'block';

                $adBlockAlertClose.addEventListener("click", function () {
                    //set cookie "adBlockClosed" for 30 days on clicking close button
                    // TODO - Need a utils file for functions that are used may time like creating cookie, dleeting cookie, etc...
                    var d = new Date(),
                        expires;
                    d.setTime(d.getTime() + (30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));
                    expires = "expires=" + d.toUTCString();
                    document.cookie = "adBlockClosed=1; " + expires;

                    $msg.className = "";
                    $msg.style.display = 'none';
                });
            }
        };

        try {
            myAdDetector === undefined; //SHOULD GIVE US AN ERROR IF ADBLOCKER IS ON AND adblock_detector.js IS BLOCKED
        } catch (e) {
            window.myAdDetector = true; //TRUE MEANS ADBLOCKER IS ACTIVATED
        }

        try {
            if (window.myAdDetector) {
                var adblockInstance = new IS_ADBLOCK();
                adblockInstance.init({
                    'wrapper': "adBlockAlertWrap",
                    'alert': "adBlockAlert",
                    'isAdblock': window.myAdDetector
                });
            }
        } catch (e) {
            console.log('is_adblock_controller: ' + e.message);
        }
    }
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
    var static_cache_ver = "3ab5f8ab79be559b4d8ffc3991cc84ee1971ab5b";
    if (!(typeof abp1 === 'undefined' && typeof abp2 === 'undefined')) {
        if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
            window.addEventListener('load', function() {
                navigator.serviceWorker.register('/service-worker.js')
                    .then(function (registration) {
                        console.log('Service Worker registration successful with scope: ', registration.scope, "   Expected version: ", static_cache_ver);

                        if (localStorage) {
                            if (localStorage.getItem("sw-version") !== static_cache_ver) {
                                try {
                                    registration.update();
                                    localStorage.setItem("sw-version", static_cache_ver);
                                    //console.log('ServiceWorker is updated to version {static_cache_ver}');
                                } catch(e) {
                                    //console.log('Service Worker needs to be updated to version {static_cache_ver}');
                                }
                            }
                        } else {
                            //do nothing. Browser will update it according to the own policy.
                        }
                    })
                    .catch(function (err) {
                        console.log(err);
                    });
            });
        }
    } else if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
        navigator.serviceWorker.getRegistrations().then(function (registrations) {
            if (registrations.length > 0) {
                for (var i = 0; i < registrations.length; i++) {
                    registrations[i].unregister();
                }
                window.location.reload(true);
            }
        });
    }
</script>

<script>
    var disclaimerZone = false;

    var showPopunder = true;

	(function() {
		//SET UP TO FILE_NAME_HERE
		var DoublePimpContent = {
			classNameToAdd: 'js-pop', //CLASS NAME TO TRIGGER THE POP UNDER PLEASE UPDATE THIS VALUE WITH THE VALUE NEEDED ON YOUR SITE
			existingNoPopUnderClass: 'removeAdLink',
			htmlElements: Tube8Utils.isGoogleChrome() ? ['.video-thumb-link'] : ['a', 'button']
		};
		var ClassNoPopunder = function(){
			"use strict";
			var Self = this;
			Self.init = function(options){
				Self.params = options;
				Self.selectHtmlElements();
			};
			Self.selectHtmlElements = function(){
				var myElements = Self.params.htmlElements;
				var i = 0;
				var j = myElements.length;
				for (; i<j; i++) {
					Self.addClassToElement(myElements[i]);
				}
			};
			// Check if element has the class name in it and if not then add the class name
			Self.checkElement = function(element) {
				if (!MG_Utils.hasClass(element, Self.params.existingNoPopUnderClass)) {
					MG_Utils.addClass(element, Self.params.classNameToAdd);
				}
			};
			Self.addClassToElement = function(element){
				var elements = document.querySelectorAll(element+':not([rel*="noopener"])');
				var i=0;
				var j=elements.length;
				for (; i<j; i++) {
					Self.checkElement(elements[i]);
				}
			};
        };

        if(showPopunder) {
            if(!disclaimerZone || (disclaimerZone && MG_Utils.getCookie("t8disclaimer") == "1")) {
                try{
                    var classNoPopUnder = new(ClassNoPopunder);
                    classNoPopUnder.init(DoublePimpContent);
                }
                catch(e){
                    console.log(e);
                }
            }
        }

		if(localStorage.getItem('g36NumFastPopsExpire')) {
			var date = new Date(localStorage.getItem('g36NumFastPopsExpire').replace('undefined__', ''));
			var now = new Date();
        }

        if(showPopunder) {
            if(!disclaimerZone || (disclaimerZone && MG_Utils.getCookie("t8disclaimer") == "1")) {

                if(!(localStorage.getItem('g36NumFastPopsExpire') && date > now) && typeof puVortexHandler !== 'undefined') {
                    var elements = document.querySelectorAll('.js-pop');
                    for (var i = 0; i < elements.length; ++i) {
                        elements[i].addEventListener("click", puVortexHandler, false);
                    }
                }

            }
        }

	})();

	if (typeof Object.assign != 'function') {
		// Must be writable: true, enumerable: false, configurable: true
		Object.defineProperty(Object, "assign", {
			value: function assign(target, varArgs) { // .length of function is 2
				'use strict';
				if (target == null) { // TypeError if undefined or null
					throw new TypeError('Cannot convert undefined or null to object');
				}

				var to = Object(target);

				for (var index = 1; index < arguments.length; index++) {
					var nextSource = arguments[index];

					if (nextSource != null) { // Skip over if undefined or null
						for (var nextKey in nextSource) {
							// Avoid bugs when hasOwnProperty is shadowed
							if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(nextSource, nextKey)) {
								to[nextKey] = nextSource[nextKey];
							}
						}
					}
				}
				return to;
			},
			writable: true,
			configurable: true
		});
